Then 102:7's direct eye-certainty supplied the far end of that staged process, and 102:8's questioning retrospectively changed `ع ل م B001` “informing” into advance notice with an evidentiary role in accountability.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B011","root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_03562c8257ad0b8e286e","text":"2. **The warning as a road-marker.** Branch evidence: ع ل م B002, distinguishing mark, landmark, road/boundary marker; ق ب ر B001/B002, grave and hiddenness; ر ء ي B011, flag or marker set up to be seen. Structural cue: this short ayah stands after the cemetery and before the repeated warning. Assigned role: علم B002 turns \"you will know\" into a marker planted on the route from graves to sight. Abductive move: I infer that the repeated warning functions as a visible boundary sign in the sequence, not only as information. Reading change: the ayah reads as a signpost placed at the cemetery edge.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001","root_001696/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0cc2bb5a05acca3c230e","text":"1. **Knowledge as interruption of diversion.** Branch evidence: ع ل م B001, knowing as a thing becomes clear; ل ه و B001, diversion; ي ق ن B001, settled knowledge with doubt removed. Structural cue: \"كلا\" breaks the previous motion; \"سوف\" projects a coming event of knowing. Assigned role: علم reverses لهو by bringing the avoided concern into clarity. Abductive move: I infer that the future knowledge concerns what التكاثر had displaced, because the warning follows immediately after the grave-limit. Reading change: the ayah reads as an interruption mechanism: clarity will arrive where distraction had operated.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0cc6938e8f1b2ee83a99","text":"1. **The first threshold in a staged disclosure.** Fixed `تَعْلَمُونَ` anchors `ع ل م B001` (a thing becoming clear to the knower). Its structural cue is the exact recurrence in 102:4 after `ثُمَّ`, followed by the verb–noun doubling `تَعْلَمُونَ عِلْمَ` in 102:5 and then two acts of seeing in 102:6–7. The first future knowing is assigned the role of initial cognitive breach in the preceding distraction. I infer that repetition plus sequencing marks distinct or deepening encounters rather than stylistic redundancy. The ayah therefore reads as the first promised stage of unavoidable clarification, not as the whole epistemic event.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_747d4cddcd43ef86b445","text":"3. **Advance notice that becomes evidence for accountability.** Fixed `ع ل م B001` also includes informing and teaching. The whole-window structure opens with `ر ح م B001` (beneficent mercy), repeats the notification, and closes with passive questioning (`س ء ل B001`) about bestowed well-being (`ن ع م B001`). Informing is assigned the role of notice given before the later interrogation. The packet supplies mercy, repeated warning, and questioning; I infer both that warning is merciful because it precedes compulsion and that repeated notice removes an ignorance defense. The future verb consequently reads in two registers at once: a threat of eventual knowing and a present act of being informed in preparation for answerability.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B012","root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_82c71b95115f6fe69665","text":"3. **Future knowledge as forced notification.** Branch evidence: ع ل م B001 includes informing; ر ء ي B012, causing someone to see; س ء ل B001, questioning. Structural cue: the surah later moves from knowing to seeing to being asked. Assigned role: علم is the first stage of compulsory disclosure; رأي and سأل intensify the same procedure. Abductive move: I infer a procedural sequence: notification precedes presentation of evidence and then interrogation. Reading change: \"you will know\" reads as the opening of an evidentiary process.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_867464d053a5574c63e0","text":"102:5 and 102:7 made this first \"سوف تعلمون\" less generic. The packet later distinguishes علم اليقين from عين اليقين, so 102:3 now reads as the first procedural notice in a series: warning-knowledge before counterfactual certainty before sight. 102:8 added a legal/audit surprise: the knowledge is not merely existential awareness of death or fire, but preparation for being questioned about a concrete field of نعيم.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9c3ff8d0de555d94d974","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:3:3\",\"root_001040\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bilme uyarısı, mezar ile doğrudan görüş arasındaki yol üzerinde yönü ve sınırı belli eden bir işaret gibi çalışır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a3152e4144faba8c0005","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:3:3\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gelecekte bilme tehdidi aynı anda şimdiden verilmiş bir bildirimdir; sonraki delil ve sorgu öncesinde bilgisizlik mazeretini daraltır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a76e410ca2fea03dd19c","text":"2. **Knowledge as a boundary marker on a misdirected route.** Fixed `ع ل م B002` supplies the dormant image of a distinguishing sign or landmark that guides toward something. Structurally, the utterance follows the grave-visit and precedes certainty and direct sight; the repeated formula itself stands like a marker placed twice at the transition. Its assigned role is orientational: it distinguishes the competitive course before the graves from what becomes manifest after them. I supply the analogy between textual repetition and a route marker, and I infer that the grave boundary makes the course newly legible. `تَعْلَمُونَ` thus gains a spatial force: the audience will not only possess information but will encounter the sign that tells them where their course has arrived.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_fedfd5a6f5f255e2880d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:3:3\",\"root_001040\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bu ilk bilme, dikkat dağınıklığını yaran ve sonraki bilgi ile görme aşamalarını başlatan ilk zorunlu açıklık eşiğidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
